# Graph Report - Pikar-Ai  (2026-09-28)

## Corpus Check
- 3066 files · ~5,993,523 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 28873 nodes · 38133 edges · 2182 communities (1958 shown, 224 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 235 edges (avg confidence: 0.73)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `0843f21c`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
